@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+## 1. 架构设计
+```mermaid
+graph TD
+    A["前端层 (React)"] --> B["3D 渲染层 (Three.js/R3F)"]
+    A --> C["UI 层 (TailwindCSS/Framer Motion)"]
+    A --> D["游戏逻辑层 (Zustand/Context)"]
+    D --> E["象棋规则引擎 (Chess Engine)"]
+    D --> F["技能系统 (Skill System)"]
+```
+
+## 2. 技术说明
+- 前端框架: React@18 + Vite
+- 3D 渲染: three, @react-three/fiber, @react-three/drei, @react-three/postprocessing
+- 样式与动画: tailwindcss@3, framer-motion (处理 UI 动画与页面过渡)
+- 状态管理: zustand (管理棋局状态、能量值、当前回合等)
+- 逻辑处理: 自定义象棋走法规则与技能特效钩子
+- 初始化工具: vite-init
+
+## 3. 路由定义
+本 Demo 作为一个单页面应用，不需要复杂的路由跳转。
+| 路由 | 目的 |
+|------|------|
+| / | 对战主界面，包含 3D 棋盘和 UI 覆盖层 |
+
+## 4. 核心逻辑结构 (TypeScript 定义参考)
+```typescript
+type Side = 'red' | 'black';
+type PieceType = 'jiang' | 'shi' | 'xiang' | 'ma' | 'che' | 'pao' | 'bing';
+
+interface Piece {
+  id: string;
+  type: PieceType;
+  side: Side;
+  position: { x: number; y: number }; // 棋盘坐标 (0-8, 0-9)
+  isAlive: boolean;
+}
+
+interface GameState {
+  board: (Piece | null)[][]; // 9x10 棋盘
+  currentTurn: Side;
+  redEnergy: number; // 0-100
+  blackEnergy: number; // 0-100
+  selectedPiece: Piece | null;
+  validMoves: { x: number; y: number }[]; // 当前选中棋子可移动的格子
+  movePiece: (from: {x: number, y: number}, to: {x: number, y: number}) => void;
+  triggerSkill: (skillId: string) => void;
+}
+```
+
+## 5. 游戏机制 (无后端)
+- **象棋规则引擎**: 纯前端计算每个棋子的合法走法，例如马的“日”字走法及别马腿检测，炮的翻山吃子逻辑等。
+- **技能系统**: 当能量满时（比如100点），玩家可以点击技能按钮，触发状态变化。例如聂无悔的“天地同寿”直接移除双方主帅，或马的“伏龙抬头”改变下一次走子特效。
+- **渲染交互**: 玩家通过点击 3D 空间内的棋子模型选中棋子，场景中高亮显示可走路径，再次点击目标格子执行走子动画。

+## 1. 产品概述
+《象棋王》Web Demo 是一款结合传统中国象棋与二次元科幻设定的 3D 网页游戏。
+- 主要目的：展示“全息投影 3D 棋盘”与“角色技能大招”相结合的核心游戏玩法。
+- 目标用户：喜欢二次元风格、策略战棋游戏和象棋的玩家。
+- 市场价值：探索传统棋类游戏在视觉与玩法创新上的可能性，验证核心玩法原型的可行性。
+
+## 2. 核心功能
+
+### 2.1 用户角色
+| 角色 | 注册方式 | 核心权限 |
+|------|----------|----------|
+| 玩家 | 访客/无需注册 | 体验完整的单机对战与技能释放流程 |
+
+### 2.2 功能模块
+1. **对战主界面 (Battle UI)**：3D悬浮棋盘、棋子交互、底部二次元UI、技能释放与状态栏。
+
+### 2.3 页面详情
+| 页面名称 | 模块名称 | 功能描述 |
+|----------|----------|----------|
+| 对战主界面 | 3D棋盘模块 | 渲染悬浮的 3D 棋盘，展示带有科技光效的棋子模型，实现象棋基础走子规则（如马走日、象飞田等）。 |
+| | 角色状态栏 | 位于屏幕底部，展示玩家角色立绘（如聂无悔）、血条与能量条（SP）。走子或吃子可积累能量。 |
+| | 技能释放区 | 位于屏幕右下角，展示带有 CD 冷却读条的技能图标。能量满时按钮闪烁，点击可释放专属绝招（如“天地同寿”、“雷霆万钧”）。 |
+
+## 3. 核心流程
+玩家进入游戏 -> 观察棋局 -> 选择己方棋子 -> 显示可移动范围 -> 移动棋子 -> 积累能量 -> 能量满时点击技能 -> 触发技能特效并改变战局。
+```mermaid
+flowchart TD
+    A["玩家进入游戏"] --> B["3D 棋盘初始化"]
+    B --> C["等待玩家操作"]
+    C --> D{"选择棋子或技能"}
+    D -->|选择棋子| E["计算并显示可移动范围"]
+    E --> F["玩家移动棋子"]
+    F --> G["执行象棋基础规则"]
+    G --> H["积累角色能量"]
+    H --> C
+    D -->|点击大招技能| I["判断能量是否满"]
+    I -->|是| J["触发角色专属技能"]
+    J --> K["执行技能特殊逻辑"]
+    K --> C
+```
+
+## 4. 用户界面设计
+### 4.1 设计风格
+- 主次色调：赛博朋克深蓝色、霓虹光效（红方使用热血橙红，黑方使用冷酷暗黑）。
+- 按钮风格：全息投影感，带有发光边框和半透明背景。
+- 字体与字号：科技感无衬线字体（如 Orbitron 风格的网页字体），关键数据采用粗体高亮。
+- 布局风格：HUD (Head-Up Display) 风格的覆盖层布局，主视图为 3D 场景，UI 贴靠底部与边角。
+- 图标风格：扁平化发光图标，带有环形进度条。
+
+### 4.2 页面设计概览
+| 页面名称 | 模块名称 | UI 元素 |
+|----------|----------|---------|
+| 对战主界面 | 3D场景 | 悬浮棋盘底座，带有网格光效；立体棋子（如使用圆柱体加上特殊材质贴图代表），移动时带有拖尾光效。 |
+| | 底部状态栏 | 左侧展示动漫风格角色头像/立绘，旁边是长条形血条（HP）和能量条（SP）。 |
+| | 技能控制区 | 右侧展示圆形技能图标，能量未满时呈灰色且显示百分比，满时呈现高亮呼吸闪烁效果。 |
+
+### 4.3 响应式设计
+以桌面端优先（Desktop-first），横屏全屏展示以获得最佳沉浸感。支持自适应缩放以兼容不同分辨率屏幕。
+
+### 4.4 3D 场景指导
+- 环境与氛围：深邃的赛博朋克暗空间，背景带有轻微的粒子漂浮物或网格线。
+- 光照设置：环境光较暗，主要依靠棋盘自发光材质（Emissive）和点光源（PointLight）营造氛围。
+- 相机设置：使用透视相机（PerspectiveCamera），默认视角为俯视 45 度角，玩家可微调旋转和缩放。
+- 材质与特效：棋子使用金属质感材质结合发光材质；走子时使用简单的补间动画（Tween）并附带发光材质切换。

+# 象棋王：绝招对决 (Xiangqi King: Ultimate Showdown)
+
+《象棋王：绝招对决》是一款融合了传统中国象棋规则与现代二次元科幻“技能大招”机制的创新网页游戏。它完全由纯前端技术打造，**单文件、免安装、完全离线**，非常适合随时随地在任何设备上游玩。
+
+## 🎮 核心玩法与特色
+
+### 1. 双模式选择
+- **人机对决 (单机)**：与内置的“天网 AI”进行对战。AI 拥有简单的价值评估逻辑，并且在能量积满时会自动释放毁灭性的技能。
+- **双人联机 (同屏)**：支持两名玩家在同一台设备（如平板、电脑屏幕）上轮流操作对弈。
+
+### 2. 能量与绝招系统 (Ultimate Skills)
+传统象棋结合了现代 RPG 的能量条机制：
+- **积攒能量 (SP)**：玩家每走一步棋增加 10 点 SP，吃掉对方棋子额外增加 10 点 SP。上限为 100 点。
+- **红方绝招【天地同寿】**：当红方能量满 100 时可手动触发。牺牲己方场上随机一个非将帅棋子，直接摧毁对方场上随机一个非将帅棋子。
+- **黑方绝招【暗影突袭】**：黑方的专属技能。人机模式下由 AI 自动触发；双人模式下黑方玩家手动触发。
+
+### 3. 科技感美术与标准棋谱
+- **赛博朋克 UI**：深色背景、发光网格、霓虹色的红黑棋子与动态呼吸的技能按钮。
+- **精准的棋盘绘制**：包含标准的楚河汉界，以及精确绘制的“炮角”和“兵角”十字/半十字标记。
+- **动态特效**：技能释放时伴随带有屏幕缩放与渐隐动画的巨大全息文字（Canvas 渲染）。
+
+### 4. 视角翻转功能 (Flip Board)
+专为双人同屏对战设计！点击界面下方的 **【🔄 视角翻转】** 按钮，棋盘将进行 180 度翻转，棋子上的汉字以及楚河汉界也会自动倒转，确保坐在屏幕对面的玩家能获得最佳视角。
+
+---
+
+## 🛠️ 技术特点与合规性
+
+- **极度轻量化 (Ultra-Lightweight)**：所有 HTML、CSS 和 JavaScript 逻辑均压缩在单一的 `index.html` 文件中，文件大小不到 20KB。
+- **完全离线 (100% Offline)**：**无任何外部依赖**。不引用外部图片、不加载第三方字体库、不需要 React/Vue/Three.js 等任何外部框架。
+- **数据持久化**：使用浏览器的 `localStorage` 在本地安全地记录您的胜负场次。
+- **高性能渲染**：游戏画面采用原生 HTML5 Canvas 绘制，保证 60fps 流畅运行；UI 层采用现代 CSS3 响应式布局。
+- **全端适配**：响应式设计完美适配手机、平板和桌面浏览器。
+
+---
+
+## 🚀 运行与使用方法
+
+1. **获取游戏**：下载本目录下的 `象棋王绝招对决.html` 文件。
+2. **启动游戏**：直接双击该 HTML 文件，使用任何现代浏览器（Chrome、Edge、Safari、Firefox 等）打开即可。
+3. **开始游玩**：
+   - 在主菜单选择模式（人机或双人）。
+   - 轮到你的回合时，点击己方棋子，棋盘会用绿色圆点高亮显示可走路径。
+   - 点击绿色圆点即可完成走子。
+   - 当下方能量条（SP）达到 100 时，技能按钮会高亮闪烁，点击即可释放绝招逆转战局！
+
+---
+
+## 📜 规则说明
+
+1. 基础走子、吃子规则完全遵循经典中国象棋规则（马走日、象走田、炮翻山等）。
+2. 红方（聂无悔）固定先行。
+3. 胜利条件：将死对方的“将”或“帅”。
+4. 任何一方释放绝招后，会直接跳过本回合，由对方继续行棋。

diff --git a/src/components/3D/Board.tsx b/src/components/3D/Board.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fdeb441
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/3D/Board.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,83 @@
+import React from 'react';
+import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
+import { Box, Plane } from '@react-three/drei';
+import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
+
+export const Board = () => {
+  const { validMoves, selectedPos } = useGameStore();
+
+  const CELL_SIZE = 1;
+  const BOARD_WIDTH = 9 * CELL_SIZE;
+  const BOARD_HEIGHT = 10 * CELL_SIZE;
+
+  // Render a 9x10 grid with holographic style
+  return (
+    <group position={[-BOARD_WIDTH / 2 + 0.5, 0, -BOARD_HEIGHT / 2 + 0.5]}>
+      {/* Base Grid */}
+      <Plane
+        args={[BOARD_WIDTH, BOARD_HEIGHT, 9, 10]}
+        rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
+        position={[BOARD_WIDTH / 2 - 0.5, -0.1, BOARD_HEIGHT / 2 - 0.5]}
+      >
+        <meshStandardMaterial color="#0ff" wireframe transparent opacity={0.2} />
+      </Plane>
+
+      {/* River separator */}
+      <Plane
+        args={[BOARD_WIDTH, CELL_SIZE]}
+        rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
+        position={[BOARD_WIDTH / 2 - 0.5, -0.05, 4.5 * CELL_SIZE]}
+      >
+        <meshBasicMaterial color="#00f" transparent opacity={0.1} />
+      </Plane>
+
+      {/* Highlight valid moves */}
+      {validMoves.map((move, idx) => (
+        <Box
+          key={`move-${idx}`}
+          args={[0.8, 0.1, 0.8]}
+          position={[move.x * CELL_SIZE, 0, move.y * CELL_SIZE]}
+          onClick={(e) => {
+            e.stopPropagation();
+            useGameStore.getState().movePiece(move.x, move.y);
+          }}
+        >
+          <meshStandardMaterial color="#0f0" transparent opacity={0.6} emissive="#0f0" emissiveIntensity={0.5} />
+        </Box>
+      ))}
+
+      {/* Highlight selected cell */}
+      {selectedPos && (
+        <Box
+          args={[0.9, 0.1, 0.9]}
+          position={[selectedPos.x * CELL_SIZE, 0, selectedPos.y * CELL_SIZE]}
+        >
+          <meshStandardMaterial color="#f0f" transparent opacity={0.5} emissive="#f0f" emissiveIntensity={1} />
+        </Box>
+      )}
+
+      {/* Empty cells for selection/movement interaction */}
+      {Array(10).fill(null).map((_, y) =>
+        Array(9).fill(null).map((_, x) => (
+          <Plane
+            key={`cell-${x}-${y}`}
+            args={[1, 1]}
+            rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
+            position={[x * CELL_SIZE, 0.05, y * CELL_SIZE]}
+            onClick={(e) => {
+              e.stopPropagation();
+              const { validMoves, movePiece } = useGameStore.getState();
+              const isValid = validMoves.some(m => m.x === x && m.y === y);
+              if (isValid) {
+                movePiece(x, y);
+              }
+            }}
+            visible={false}
+          >
+            <meshBasicMaterial transparent opacity={0} />
+          </Plane>
+        ))
+      )}
+    </group>
+  );
+};
diff --git a/src/components/3D/GameScene.tsx b/src/components/3D/GameScene.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..5524365
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/3D/GameScene.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,57 @@
+import React, { Suspense, useMemo } from 'react';
+import { Canvas } from '@react-three/fiber';
+import { OrbitControls, Environment, Stars, PerspectiveCamera } from '@react-three/drei';
+import { EffectComposer, Bloom, ChromaticAberration } from '@react-three/postprocessing';
+import { Board } from './Board';
+import { Piece } from './Piece';
+import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
+import * as THREE from 'three';
+
+export const GameScene = () => {
+  const { board, selectedPos, selectPiece } = useGameStore();
+  const chromaticOffset = useMemo(() => new THREE.Vector2(0.002, 0.002), []);
+
+  return (
+    <div className="w-full h-full bg-black">
+      <Canvas shadows camera={{ position: [0, 8, 10], fov: 45 }}>
+        <color attach="background" args={['#050510']} />
+        <ambientLight intensity={0.2} />
+        <directionalLight position={[5, 10, 5]} intensity={1} castShadow />
+        <pointLight position={[0, 5, 0]} intensity={0.5} color="#0ff" />
+        
+        <Stars radius={100} depth={50} count={5000} factor={4} saturation={0} fade speed={1} />
+        
+        <Suspense fallback={null}>
+          <Board />
+          {board.map((row, y) =>
+            row.map((piece, x) =>
+              piece ? (
+                <Piece
+                  key={piece.id}
+                  piece={piece}
+                  x={x}
+                  y={y}
+                  isSelected={selectedPos?.x === x && selectedPos?.y === y}
+                  onClick={() => selectPiece(x, y)}
+                />
+              ) : null
+            )
+          )}
+        </Suspense>
+
+        <OrbitControls
+          enablePan={false}
+          maxPolarAngle={Math.PI / 2.2}
+          minPolarAngle={Math.PI / 6}
+          minDistance={5}
+          maxDistance={15}
+        />
+        
+        <EffectComposer>
+          <Bloom luminanceThreshold={0.2} luminanceSmoothing={0.9} height={300} intensity={1.5} />
+          <ChromaticAberration offset={chromaticOffset} radialModulation={false} modulationOffset={0} />
+        </EffectComposer>
+      </Canvas>
+    </div>
+  );
+};
diff --git a/src/components/3D/Piece.tsx b/src/components/3D/Piece.tsx
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..d62355b
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/components/3D/Piece.tsx
@@ -0,0 +1,100 @@
+import React, { useRef } from 'react';
+import { useFrame } from '@react-three/fiber';
+import { Text, Cylinder } from '@react-three/drei';
+import { Piece as PieceType } from '../../utils/chessLogic';
+import * as THREE from 'three';
+
+interface PieceProps {
+  piece: PieceType;
+  x: number;
+  y: number;
+  isSelected: boolean;
+  onClick: () => void;
+}
+
+const PIECE_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
+  'jiang': '将',
+  'shi': '士',
+  'xiang': '象',
+  'ma': '马',
+  'che': '车',
+  'pao': '炮',
+  'bing': '兵',
+};
+
+const RED_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
+  'jiang': '帅',
+  'shi': '仕',
+  'xiang': '相',
+  'ma': '马',
+  'che': '车',
+  'pao': '炮',
+  'bing': '兵',
+};
+
+export const Piece = ({ piece, x, y, isSelected, onClick }: PieceProps) => {
+  const meshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh>(null);
+  
+  const CELL_SIZE = 1;
+  const BOARD_WIDTH = 9 * CELL_SIZE;
+  const BOARD_HEIGHT = 10 * CELL_SIZE;
+  
+  const posX = x * CELL_SIZE - BOARD_WIDTH / 2 + 0.5;
+  const posZ = y * CELL_SIZE - BOARD_HEIGHT / 2 + 0.5;
+  const posY = isSelected ? 0.5 : 0.2;
+
+  useFrame((state) => {
+    if (!meshRef.current) return;
+    
+    // Smooth transition
+    meshRef.current.position.x = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(meshRef.current.position.x, posX, 0.1);
+    meshRef.current.position.z = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(meshRef.current.position.z, posZ, 0.1);
+    meshRef.current.position.y = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(meshRef.current.position.y, posY, 0.1);
+
+    if (isSelected) {
+      meshRef.current.rotation.y += 0.05;
+    } else {
+      meshRef.current.rotation.y = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(meshRef.current.rotation.y, 0, 0.1);
+    }
+  });
+
+  const isRed = piece.side === 'red';
+  const color = isRed ? '#ff3333' : '#33ccff';
+  const emissiveColor = isRed ? '#ff0000' : '#00ffff';
+  const label = isRed ? RED_LABELS[piece.type] : PIECE_LABELS[piece.type];
+
+  return (
+    <group onClick={(e) => { e.stopPropagation(); onClick(); }}>
+      <Cylinder
+        ref={meshRef}
+        args={[0.4, 0.4, 0.2, 32]}
+        position={[posX, posY, posZ]}
+      >
+        <meshStandardMaterial
+          color={color}
+          emissive={emissiveColor}
+          emissiveIntensity={isSelected ? 1.5 : 0.5}
+          metalness={0.8}
+          roughness={0.2}
+        />
+        
+        {/* Inner ring */}
+        <Cylinder args={[0.3, 0.3, 0.22, 32]} position={[0, 0, 0]}>
+          <meshStandardMaterial color="#111" metalness={0.9} roughness={0.1} />
+        </Cylinder>
+
+        {/* Text Label */}
+        <Text
+          position={[0, 0.12, 0]}
+          rotation={[-Math.PI / 2, 0, 0]}
+          fontSize={0.4}
+          color={color}
+          anchorX="center"
+          anchorY="middle"
+        >
+          {label}
+        </Text>
+      </Cylinder>
+    </group>
+  );
+};

+import React from 'react';
+import { useGameStore } from '../../store/gameStore';
+import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion';
+import { Zap, Shield, Swords } from 'lucide-react';
+
+export const BattleUI = () => {
+  const { currentTurn, redEnergy, blackEnergy, triggerSkill } = useGameStore();
+
+  const isRedTurn = currentTurn === 'red';
+
+  return (
+    <div className="absolute inset-0 pointer-events-none flex flex-col justify-between p-6 z-10">
+      {/* Top Header */}
+      <div className="flex justify-between items-start w-full">
+        <div className="bg-black/50 border border-cyan-500/30 p-4 rounded-xl backdrop-blur-md shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,255,255,0.2)] pointer-events-auto">
+          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
+            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-gray-800 rounded-full border-2 border-cyan-500 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center relative">
+              {/* Opponent Avatar placeholder */}
+              <div className="text-cyan-500 text-xl font-bold">对手</div>
+              {!isRedTurn && (
+                <motion.div
+                  className="absolute inset-0 border-4 border-cyan-500 rounded-full"
+                  animate={{ scale: [1, 1.2, 1], opacity: [1, 0, 1] }}
+                  transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: 1.5 }}
+                />
+              )}
+            </div>
+            <div>
+              <h2 className="text-cyan-400 font-bold text-xl tracking-wider">天网 AI</h2>
+              <div className="w-48 h-3 bg-gray-900 rounded-full mt-2 overflow-hidden border border-gray-700">
+                <div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-600 to-cyan-400 w-full" />
+              </div>
+              <div className="w-48 h-2 bg-gray-900 rounded-full mt-1 overflow-hidden border border-gray-700 relative">
+                <motion.div
+                  className="h-full bg-cyan-500"
+                  initial={{ width: `${blackEnergy}%` }}
+                  animate={{ width: `${blackEnergy}%` }}
+                />
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        <div className="bg-black/50 border border-yellow-500/30 px-6 py-2 rounded-full backdrop-blur-md pointer-events-auto">
+          <span className="text-yellow-500 font-bold tracking-widest">
+            {isRedTurn ? '红方回合' : '黑方回合'}
+          </span>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+
+      {/* Bottom Footer */}
+      <div className="flex justify-between items-end">
+        {/* Player Info */}
+        <div className="bg-black/60 border-t-2 border-r-2 border-red-500/50 p-6 rounded-tr-3xl backdrop-blur-lg shadow-[5px_-5px_20px_rgba(255,0,0,0.15)] pointer-events-auto relative overflow-hidden">
+          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(45deg,transparent_25%,rgba(255,0,0,0.05)_50%,transparent_75%,transparent_100%)] bg-[length:250px_250px] animate-[gradient_3s_linear_infinite]" />
+          
+          <div className="flex items-end space-x-6 relative z-10">
+            <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-gray-800 rounded-2xl border-2 border-red-500 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center relative shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(255,0,0,0.4)]">
+              {/* Protagonist Avatar placeholder */}
+              <div className="text-red-500 text-2xl font-bold">无悔</div>
+              {isRedTurn && (
+                <motion.div
+                  className="absolute inset-0 border-4 border-red-500 rounded-2xl"
+                  animate={{ scale: [1, 1.1, 1], opacity: [1, 0, 1] }}
+                  transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: 1.5 }}
+                />
+              )}
+            </div>
+            <div className="pb-2">
+              <h2 className="text-red-400 font-bold text-3xl tracking-wider uppercase drop-shadow-[0_0_5px_rgba(255,0,0,0.8)]">聂无悔</h2>
+              <div className="flex items-center mt-3 space-x-2">
+                <span className="text-gray-400 text-xs font-bold w-6">HP</span>
+                <div className="w-64 h-4 bg-gray-900 rounded-sm overflow-hidden border border-gray-700 shadow-inner">
+                  <div className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-red-600 to-red-400 w-[100%]" />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+              <div className="flex items-center mt-2 space-x-2">
+                <span className="text-cyan-400 text-xs font-bold w-6">SP</span>
+                <div className="w-64 h-3 bg-gray-900 rounded-sm overflow-hidden border border-gray-700 relative shadow-inner">
+                  <motion.div
+                    className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-cyan-400 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(0,255,255,0.8)]"
+                    initial={{ width: `${redEnergy}%` }}
+                    animate={{ width: `${redEnergy}%` }}
+                    transition={{ type: 'spring', bounce: 0.25 }}
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Skill Controls */}
+        <div className="pointer-events-auto flex space-x-6 items-end pb-4 pr-4">
+          <SkillButton
+            icon={<Shield size={24} />}
+            name="固若金汤"
+            cooldown={50}
+            active={false}
+            onClick={() => {}}
+          />
+          <SkillButton
+            icon={<Swords size={24} />}
+            name="伏龙抬头"
+            cooldown={80}
+            active={false}
+            onClick={() => {}}
+          />
+          <div className="relative">
+            <AnimatePresence>
+              {redEnergy >= 100 && (
+                <motion.div
+                  initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8 }}
+                  animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
+                  exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.8 }}
+                  className="absolute -top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-red-500 font-bold tracking-widest text-lg whitespace-nowrap drop-shadow-[0_0_10px_rgba(255,0,0,1)]"
+                >
+                  绝招就绪!
+                </motion.div>
+              )}
+            </AnimatePresence>
+            <button
+              onClick={() => triggerSkill('red')}
+              disabled={redEnergy < 100}
+              className={`relative w-24 h-24 rounded-full border-4 flex flex-col items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300
+                ${redEnergy >= 100 
+                  ? 'bg-red-900/80 border-red-500 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(255,0,0,0.6)] hover:bg-red-800 hover:scale-110 cursor-pointer' 
+                  : 'bg-gray-900/80 border-gray-700 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed grayscale'}`}
+            >
+              {redEnergy >= 100 && (
+                <motion.div
+                  className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full border-4 border-red-500"
+                  animate={{ scale: [1, 1.3], opacity: [1, 0] }}
+                  transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: 1 }}
+                />
+              )}
+              <Zap size={32} className={redEnergy >= 100 ? 'text-yellow-400 drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(255,255,0,0.8)]' : ''} />
+              <span className={`text-xs mt-1 font-bold ${redEnergy >= 100 ? 'text-white' : ''}`}>天地同寿</span>
+              
+              {redEnergy < 100 && (
+                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 rounded-full backdrop-blur-sm">
+                  <span className="text-xl font-bold text-white drop-shadow-md">{redEnergy}%</span>
+                </div>
+              )}
+            </button>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+};
+
+const SkillButton = ({ icon, name, cooldown, active, onClick }: { icon: React.ReactNode, name: string, cooldown: number, active: boolean, onClick: () => void }) => {
+  return (
+    <button
+      onClick={onClick}
+      disabled={!active}
+      className={`relative w-16 h-16 rounded-full border-2 flex flex-col items-center justify-center transition-all duration-300
+        ${active 
+          ? 'bg-blue-900/60 border-cyan-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,255,255,0.4)] hover:bg-blue-800 cursor-pointer text-cyan-300' 
+          : 'bg-gray-900/80 border-gray-700 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed'}`}
+    >
+      {icon}
+      <span className="text-[10px] mt-1 scale-75 whitespace-nowrap">{name}</span>
+      {!active && (
+        <svg className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full -rotate-90">
+          <circle
+            cx="32"
+            cy="32"
+            r="30"
+            fill="none"
+            stroke="rgba(0,255,255,0.3)"
+            strokeWidth="4"
+            strokeDasharray="188.5"
+            strokeDashoffset={188.5 * (1 - cooldown / 100)}
+            className="transition-all duration-300"
+          />
+        </svg>
+      )}
+    </button>
+  );
+};
diff --git a/src/hooks/useTheme.ts b/src/hooks/useTheme.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..841f812
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/hooks/useTheme.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,29 @@
+import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
+
+type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';
+
+export function useTheme() {
+  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(() => {
+    const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('theme') as Theme;
+    if (savedTheme) {
+      return savedTheme;
+    }
+    return window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light';
+  });
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    document.documentElement.classList.remove('light', 'dark');
+    document.documentElement.classList.add(theme);
+    localStorage.setItem('theme', theme);
+  }, [theme]);
+
+  const toggleTheme = () => {
+    setTheme(prevTheme => prevTheme === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light');
+  };
+
+  return {
+    theme,
+    toggleTheme,
+    isDark: theme === 'dark'
+  };
+}

diff --git a/src/store/gameStore.ts b/src/store/gameStore.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..c76f045
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/store/gameStore.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,95 @@
+import { create } from 'zustand';
+import { BoardState, getInitialBoard, getValidMoves, Piece, Side } from '../utils/chessLogic';
+
+interface GameState {
+  board: BoardState;
+  currentTurn: Side;
+  redEnergy: number;
+  blackEnergy: number;
+  selectedPos: { x: number; y: number } | null;
+  validMoves: { x: number; y: number }[];
+  selectPiece: (x: number, y: number) => void;
+  movePiece: (toX: number, toY: number) => void;
+  triggerSkill: (side: Side) => void;
+  resetGame: () => void;
+}
+
+export const useGameStore = create<GameState>((set, get) => ({
+  board: getInitialBoard(),
+  currentTurn: 'red',
+  redEnergy: 0,
+  blackEnergy: 0,
+  selectedPos: null,
+  validMoves: [],
+
+  selectPiece: (x: number, y: number) => {
+    const { board, currentTurn } = get();
+    const piece = board[y][x];
+
+    if (piece && piece.side === currentTurn) {
+      const moves = getValidMoves(board, x, y);
+      set({ selectedPos: { x, y }, validMoves: moves });
+    } else {
+      set({ selectedPos: null, validMoves: [] });
+    }
+  },
+
+  movePiece: (toX: number, toY: number) => {
+    const { board, selectedPos, currentTurn, validMoves, redEnergy, blackEnergy } = get();
+    if (!selectedPos) return;
+
+    // Check if move is valid
+    const isValid = validMoves.some(m => m.x === toX && m.y === toY);
+    if (!isValid) return;
+
+    const newBoard = board.map(row => [...row]);
+    const piece = newBoard[selectedPos.y][selectedPos.x];
+    const targetPiece = newBoard[toY][toX];
+
+    // Move
+    newBoard[toY][toX] = piece;
+    newBoard[selectedPos.y][selectedPos.x] = null;
+
+    // Energy logic
+    let newRedEnergy = redEnergy;
+    let newBlackEnergy = blackEnergy;
+
+    if (currentTurn === 'red') {
+      newRedEnergy = Math.min(100, newRedEnergy + 10);
+      if (targetPiece) newRedEnergy = Math.min(100, newRedEnergy + 20);
+    } else {
+      newBlackEnergy = Math.min(100, newBlackEnergy + 10);
+      if (targetPiece) newBlackEnergy = Math.min(100, newBlackEnergy + 20);
+    }
+
+    set({
+      board: newBoard,
+      currentTurn: currentTurn === 'red' ? 'black' : 'red',
+      selectedPos: null,
+      validMoves: [],
+      redEnergy: newRedEnergy,
+      blackEnergy: newBlackEnergy,
+    });
+  },
+
+  triggerSkill: (side: Side) => {
+    const { redEnergy, blackEnergy } = get();
+    if (side === 'red' && redEnergy >= 100) {
+      // Trigger special effect (reset energy, add visual effect later)
+      set({ redEnergy: 0 });
+    } else if (side === 'black' && blackEnergy >= 100) {
+      set({ blackEnergy: 0 });
+    }
+  },
+
+  resetGame: () => {
+    set({
+      board: getInitialBoard(),
+      currentTurn: 'red',
+      redEnergy: 0,
+      blackEnergy: 0,
+      selectedPos: null,
+      validMoves: [],
+    });
+  },
+}));
diff --git a/src/utils/chessLogic.ts b/src/utils/chessLogic.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..f17b1c1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/utils/chessLogic.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,261 @@
+export type Side = 'red' | 'black';
+export type PieceType = 'jiang' | 'shi' | 'xiang' | 'ma' | 'che' | 'pao' | 'bing';
+
+export interface Piece {
+  id: string;
+  type: PieceType;
+  side: Side;
+}
+
+export type BoardState = (Piece | null)[][];
+
+const createPiece = (type: PieceType, side: Side): Piece => ({
+  id: `${side}-${type}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(7)}`,
+  type,
+  side,
+});
+
+export const getInitialBoard = (): BoardState => {
+  const board: BoardState = Array(10).fill(null).map(() => Array(9).fill(null));
+
+  // Black pieces (top, y = 0-3)
+  board[0][0] = createPiece('che', 'black');
+  board[0][1] = createPiece('ma', 'black');
+  board[0][2] = createPiece('xiang', 'black');
+  board[0][3] = createPiece('shi', 'black');
+  board[0][4] = createPiece('jiang', 'black');
+  board[0][5] = createPiece('shi', 'black');
+  board[0][6] = createPiece('xiang', 'black');
+  board[0][7] = createPiece('ma', 'black');
+  board[0][8] = createPiece('che', 'black');
+
+  board[2][1] = createPiece('pao', 'black');
+  board[2][7] = createPiece('pao', 'black');
+
+  board[3][0] = createPiece('bing', 'black');
+  board[3][2] = createPiece('bing', 'black');
+  board[3][4] = createPiece('bing', 'black');
+  board[3][6] = createPiece('bing', 'black');
+  board[3][8] = createPiece('bing', 'black');
+
+  // Red pieces (bottom, y = 6-9)
+  board[9][0] = createPiece('che', 'red');
+  board[9][1] = createPiece('ma', 'red');
+  board[9][2] = createPiece('xiang', 'red');
+  board[9][3] = createPiece('shi', 'red');
+  board[9][4] = createPiece('jiang', 'red');
+  board[9][5] = createPiece('shi', 'red');
+  board[9][6] = createPiece('xiang', 'red');
+  board[9][7] = createPiece('ma', 'red');
+  board[9][8] = createPiece('che', 'red');
+
+  board[7][1] = createPiece('pao', 'red');
+  board[7][7] = createPiece('pao', 'red');
+
+  board[6][0] = createPiece('bing', 'red');
+  board[6][2] = createPiece('bing', 'red');
+  board[6][4] = createPiece('bing', 'red');
+  board[6][6] = createPiece('bing', 'red');
+  board[6][8] = createPiece('bing', 'red');
+
+  return board;
+};
+
+// Valid move logic
+export const getValidMoves = (board: BoardState, x: number, y: number): { x: number; y: number }[] => {
+  const piece = board[y][x];
+  if (!piece) return [];
+
+  const moves: { x: number; y: number }[] = [];
+  const side = piece.side;
+
+  const addMove = (nx: number, ny: number) => {
+    if (nx >= 0 && nx < 9 && ny >= 0 && ny < 10) {
+      const target = board[ny][nx];
+      if (!target || target.side !== side) {
+        moves.push({ x: nx, y: ny });
+      }
+    }
+  };
+
+  if (piece.type === 'che') {
+    // Up
+    for (let i = y - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+      addMove(x, i);
+      if (board[i][x]) break;
+    }
+    // Down
+    for (let i = y + 1; i < 10; i++) {
+      addMove(x, i);
+      if (board[i][x]) break;
+    }
+    // Left
+    for (let i = x - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+      addMove(i, y);
+      if (board[y][i]) break;
+    }
+    // Right
+    for (let i = x + 1; i < 9; i++) {
+      addMove(i, y);
+      if (board[y][i]) break;
+    }
+  }
+
+  // Basic move rules for a demo. Real rules (like Ma blocking) can be fully implemented or simplified.
+  if (piece.type === 'ma') {
+    const directions = [
+      { dx: 1, dy: -2, blockX: 0, blockY: -1 },
+      { dx: 2, dy: -1, blockX: 1, blockY: 0 },
+      { dx: 2, dy: 1, blockX: 1, blockY: 0 },
+      { dx: 1, dy: 2, blockX: 0, blockY: 1 },
+      { dx: -1, dy: 2, blockX: 0, blockY: 1 },
+      { dx: -2, dy: 1, blockX: -1, blockY: 0 },
+      { dx: -2, dy: -1, blockX: -1, blockY: 0 },
+      { dx: -1, dy: -2, blockX: 0, blockY: -1 },
+    ];
+    directions.forEach(({ dx, dy, blockX, blockY }) => {
+      const bx = x + blockX;
+      const by = y + blockY;
+      if (bx >= 0 && bx < 9 && by >= 0 && by < 10 && !board[by][bx]) {
+        addMove(x + dx, y + dy);
+      }
+    });
+  }
+
+  if (piece.type === 'xiang') {
+    const directions = [
+      { dx: 2, dy: -2, blockX: 1, blockY: -1 },
+      { dx: 2, dy: 2, blockX: 1, blockY: 1 },
+      { dx: -2, dy: 2, blockX: -1, blockY: 1 },
+      { dx: -2, dy: -2, blockX: -1, blockY: -1 },
+    ];
+    directions.forEach(({ dx, dy, blockX, blockY }) => {
+      const bx = x + blockX;
+      const by = y + blockY;
+      const nx = x + dx;
+      const ny = y + dy;
+      // Xiang cannot cross river
+      if (side === 'red' && ny < 5) return;
+      if (side === 'black' && ny > 4) return;
+      
+      if (bx >= 0 && bx < 9 && by >= 0 && by < 10 && !board[by][bx]) {
+        addMove(nx, ny);
+      }
+    });
+  }
+
+  if (piece.type === 'shi') {
+    const directions = [
+      { dx: 1, dy: 1 }, { dx: 1, dy: -1 }, { dx: -1, dy: 1 }, { dx: -1, dy: -1 }
+    ];
+    directions.forEach(({ dx, dy }) => {
+      const nx = x + dx;
+      const ny = y + dy;
+      if (nx >= 3 && nx <= 5) {
+        if (side === 'red' && ny >= 7 && ny <= 9) addMove(nx, ny);
+        if (side === 'black' && ny >= 0 && ny <= 2) addMove(nx, ny);
+      }
+    });
+  }
+
+  if (piece.type === 'jiang') {
+    const directions = [
+      { dx: 0, dy: 1 }, { dx: 0, dy: -1 }, { dx: 1, dy: 0 }, { dx: -1, dy: 0 }
+    ];
+    directions.forEach(({ dx, dy }) => {
+      const nx = x + dx;
+      const ny = y + dy;
+      if (nx >= 3 && nx <= 5) {
+        if (side === 'red' && ny >= 7 && ny <= 9) addMove(nx, ny);
+        if (side === 'black' && ny >= 0 && ny <= 2) addMove(nx, ny);
+      }
+    });
+    // Flying general
+    // (Simplified logic: we can skip for demo or just add basic move)
+  }
+
+  if (piece.type === 'bing') {
+    if (side === 'red') {
+      addMove(x, y - 1); // Move forward
+      if (y < 5) { // Crossed river
+        addMove(x - 1, y);
+        addMove(x + 1, y);
+      }
+    } else {
+      addMove(x, y + 1); // Move forward
+      if (y > 4) { // Crossed river
+        addMove(x - 1, y);
+        addMove(x + 1, y);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (piece.type === 'pao') {
+    // Up
+    let foundPiece = false;
+    for (let i = y - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+      if (!foundPiece) {
+        if (board[i][x]) {
+          foundPiece = true;
+        } else {
+          addMove(x, i);
+        }
+      } else {
+        if (board[i][x]) {
+          if (board[i][x]?.side !== side) addMove(x, i);
+          break;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    // Down
+    foundPiece = false;
+    for (let i = y + 1; i < 10; i++) {
+      if (!foundPiece) {
+        if (board[i][x]) {
+          foundPiece = true;
+        } else {
+          addMove(x, i);
+        }
+      } else {
+        if (board[i][x]) {
+          if (board[i][x]?.side !== side) addMove(x, i);
+          break;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    // Left
+    foundPiece = false;
+    for (let i = x - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+      if (!foundPiece) {
+        if (board[y][i]) {
+          foundPiece = true;
+        } else {
+          addMove(i, y);
+        }
+      } else {
+        if (board[y][i]) {
+          if (board[y][i]?.side !== side) addMove(i, y);
+          break;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    // Right
+    foundPiece = false;
+    for (let i = x + 1; i < 9; i++) {
+      if (!foundPiece) {
+        if (board[y][i]) {
+          foundPiece = true;
+        } else {
+          addMove(i, y);
+        }
+      } else {
+        if (board[y][i]) {
+          if (board[y][i]?.side !== side) addMove(i, y);
+          break;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  return moves;
+};

+    </div>

+<!-- 规则弹窗 -->
+<div id="help-modal" class="modal">
+    <div class="modal-content">
+        <h2>绝招对决 规则</h2>
+        <p>
+            1. 经典中国象棋规则，红方先行。<br>

+            3. <strong>红方绝招【天地同寿】</strong>：能量满时点击发动，牺牲己方场上随机1个非将帅棋子，直接摧毁对方场上随机1个非将帅棋子。<br>
+            4. <strong>黑方绝招【暗影突袭】</strong>：效果同上。在人机模式中，AI满能量时会自动发动。<br>
+            5. <strong>双人同屏</strong>：支持两人在同一设备轮流操作，可使用“视角翻转”按钮互换棋盘方向！
+        </p>
+        <button id="btn-close-help" class="btn" style="width: 100%">我明白了</button>

+
+<script>
+/**
+ * 象棋王：绝招对决 v2.0
+ * 包含：单机AI、双人同屏联机、精准棋盘绘制、视角翻转
+ */
+
+(function() {
+    // --- 核心配置与状态 ---
+    const CONFIG = {
+        boardCols: 9,
+        boardRows: 10,
+        colors: {
+            board: '#0f172a', grid: '#1e293b', gridLine: '#38bdf8',
+            red: '#ef4444', redGlow: 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.6)',
+            black: '#94a3b8', blackGlow: 'rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.6)',
+            highlight: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)',
+            select: 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.6)',
+            validMove: 'rgba(34, 197, 94, 0.6)',
+            boardText: 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.4)'
+        }
+    };
+
+    let state = {
+        mode: 'ai', // 'ai' | 'pvp'
+        board: [], // 10x9 数组。0行为黑底线，9行为红底线。
+        turn: 'red',
+        selectedPiece: null,
+        validMoves: [],
+        history: [],
+        gameOver: false,
+        redEnergy: 0,
+        blackEnergy: 0,
+        stats: { wins: 0, losses: 0 },
+        isFlipped: false // true 则黑方在下，红方在上
+    };
+
+    // DOM 元素
+    const UI = {
+        menu: document.getElementById('main-menu'),
+        game: document.getElementById('game-container'),
+        canvas: document.getElementById('game-canvas'),
+        ctx: document.getElementById('game-canvas').getContext('2d'),
+        effectCanvas: document.getElementById('skill-effect'),
+        effectCtx: document.getElementById('skill-effect').getContext('2d'),
+        msg: document.getElementById('message-overlay'),
+        modeTitle: document.getElementById('game-mode-title'),
+        blackName: document.getElementById('black-name'),
+        btnRedSkill: document.getElementById('btn-red-skill'),
+        btnBlackSkill: document.getElementById('btn-black-skill')
+    };
+
+    const PIECES = {
+        'r_jiang': { text: '帅', type: 'jiang', color: 'red' },
+        'r_shi': { text: '仕', type: 'shi', color: 'red' },
+        'r_xiang': { text: '相', type: 'xiang', color: 'red' },
+        'r_ma': { text: '马', type: 'ma', color: 'red' },
+        'r_che': { text: '车', type: 'che', color: 'red' },
+        'r_pao': { text: '炮', type: 'pao', color: 'red' },
+        'r_bing': { text: '兵', type: 'bing', color: 'red' },
+        
+        'b_jiang': { text: '将', type: 'jiang', color: 'black' },
+        'b_shi': { text: '士', type: 'shi', color: 'black' },
+        'b_xiang': { text: '象', type: 'xiang', color: 'black' },
+        'b_ma': { text: '马', type: 'ma', color: 'black' },
+        'b_che': { text: '车', type: 'che', color: 'black' },
+        'b_pao': { text: '炮', type: 'pao', color: 'black' },
+        'b_bing': { text: '卒', type: 'bing', color: 'black' }
+    };
+
+    // --- 初始化 ---
+
+    function init() {
+        loadStats();
+        window.addEventListener('resize', resizeCanvas);
+        
+        UI.canvas.addEventListener('click', handleBoardClick);
+        
+        // 菜单事件
+        document.getElementById('btn-start-ai').addEventListener('click', () => startGame('ai'));
+        document.getElementById('btn-start-pvp').addEventListener('click', () => startGame('pvp'));
+        
+        // 游戏控制事件
+        document.getElementById('btn-restart').addEventListener('click', () => startGame(state.mode));
+        document.getElementById('btn-undo').addEventListener('click', undoMove);
+        document.getElementById('btn-flip').addEventListener('click', () => {
+            state.isFlipped = !state.isFlipped;
+            drawBoard();
+        });
+        document.getElementById('btn-exit').addEventListener('click', () => {
+            UI.game.style.display = 'none';
+            UI.menu.style.display = 'flex';
+        });
+        
+        // 技能事件
+        UI.btnRedSkill.addEventListener('click', () => useSkill('red'));
+        UI.btnBlackSkill.addEventListener('click', () => useSkill('black'));
+        
+        // 规则说明
+        const showHelp = () => document.getElementById('help-modal').style.display = 'flex';
+        document.getElementById('btn-menu-help').addEventListener('click', showHelp);
+        document.getElementById('btn-close-help').addEventListener('click', () => {
+            document.getElementById('help-modal').style.display = 'none';
+        });
+
+        requestAnimationFrame(renderLoop);
+    }
+
+    function loadStats() {
+        try {
+            state.stats.wins = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('xw_wins') || 0);
+            state.stats.losses = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('xw_losses') || 0);
+            updateStatsUI();
+        } catch(e) {}
+    }
+
+    function saveStats() {
+        try {
+            localStorage.setItem('xw_wins', state.stats.wins);
+            localStorage.setItem('xw_losses', state.stats.losses);
+            updateStatsUI();
+        } catch(e) {}
+    }
+
+    function updateStatsUI() {
+        document.getElementById('win-count').textContent = state.stats.wins;
+        document.getElementById('loss-count').textContent = state.stats.losses;
+    }
+
+    function resizeCanvas() {
+        if (UI.game.style.display === 'none') return;
+        
+        const container = document.getElementById('board-container');
+        const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();
+        const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
+        
+        UI.canvas.width = rect.width * dpr;
+        UI.canvas.height = rect.height * dpr;
+        UI.effectCanvas.width = rect.width * dpr;
+        UI.effectCanvas.height = rect.height * dpr;
+        
+        UI.ctx.scale(dpr, dpr);
+        UI.effectCtx.scale(dpr, dpr);
+        
+        CONFIG.cellSize = Math.min(rect.width / 10, rect.height / 11);
+        CONFIG.offsetX = (rect.width - CONFIG.cellSize * 8) / 2;
+        CONFIG.offsetY = (rect.height - CONFIG.cellSize * 9) / 2;
+        
+        drawBoard();
+    }
+
+    function startGame(mode) {
+        state.mode = mode;
+        state.isFlipped = false;
+        
+        UI.menu.style.display = 'none';
+        UI.game.style.display = 'flex';
+        
+        if (mode === 'ai') {
+            UI.modeTitle.textContent = "人机对决";
+            UI.blackName.textContent = "天网 AI (黑方)";
+            UI.btnBlackSkill.style.display = 'none'; // AI自动放技能
+        } else {
+            UI.modeTitle.textContent = "双人同屏";
+            UI.blackName.textContent = "玩家2 (黑方)";
+            UI.btnBlackSkill.style.display = 'block';
+        }
+
+        // 初始化标准棋盘 (0行为黑底线，9行为红底线)
+        state.board = Array(10).fill(null).map(() => Array(9).fill(null));
+        state.board[0] = ['b_che', 'b_ma', 'b_xiang', 'b_shi', 'b_jiang', 'b_shi', 'b_xiang', 'b_ma', 'b_che'];
+        state.board[2][1] = 'b_pao'; state.board[2][7] = 'b_pao';
+        state.board[3][0] = 'b_bing'; state.board[3][2] = 'b_bing'; state.board[3][4] = 'b_bing'; state.board[3][6] = 'b_bing'; state.board[3][8] = 'b_bing';
+
+        state.board[9] = ['r_che', 'r_ma', 'r_xiang', 'r_shi', 'r_jiang', 'r_shi', 'r_xiang', 'r_ma', 'r_che'];
+        state.board[7][1] = 'r_pao'; state.board[7][7] = 'r_pao';
+        state.board[6][0] = 'r_bing'; state.board[6][2] = 'r_bing'; state.board[6][4] = 'r_bing'; state.board[6][6] = 'r_bing'; state.board[6][8] = 'r_bing';
+
+        state.turn = 'red';
+        state.selectedPiece = null;
+        state.validMoves = [];
+        state.history = [];
+        state.gameOver = false;
+        state.redEnergy = 0;
+        state.blackEnergy = 0;
+        
+        resizeCanvas();
+        updateUI();
+        showMessage(mode === 'ai' ? "对决开始！" : "双人联机！", 1500);
+    }
+
+    function saveHistory() {
+        state.history.push({
+            board: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state.board)),
+            redEnergy: state.redEnergy,
+            blackEnergy: state.blackEnergy,
+            turn: state.turn
+        });
+    }
+
+    function undoMove() {
+        if (state.gameOver || state.history.length === 0) return;
+        
+        if (state.mode === 'ai') {
+            // 人机模式回退两步
+            if (state.history.length < 2 || state.turn !== 'red') return;
+            state.history.pop();
+            const prev = state.history.pop();
+            restoreState(prev);
+        } else {
+            // PvP模式回退一步
+            const prev = state.history.pop();
+            restoreState(prev);
+        }
+        updateUI();
+    }
+
+    function restoreState(prevState) {
+        state.board = prevState.board;
+        state.redEnergy = prevState.redEnergy;
+        state.blackEnergy = prevState.blackEnergy;
+        state.turn = prevState.turn;
+        state.selectedPiece = null;
+        state.validMoves = [];
+    }
+
+    // --- 坐标转换 (支持视角翻转) ---
+    
+    // 逻辑坐标 -> 渲染坐标
+    function getRenderPos(r, c) {
+        return state.isFlipped ? { r: 9 - r, c: 8 - c } : { r, c };
+    }
+    
+    // 渲染坐标 -> 逻辑坐标
+    function getLogicPos(renderR, renderC) {
+        return state.isFlipped ? { r: 9 - renderR, c: 8 - renderC } : { r: renderR, c: renderC };
+    }
+
+    // --- 游戏规则逻辑 ---
+
+    function getPieceColor(id) { return id ? (id.startsWith('r_') ? 'red' : 'black') : null; }
+    function getPieceType(id) { return id ? id.substring(2) : null; }
+
+    function calculateValidMoves(r, c) {
+        const pieceId = state.board[r][c];
+        if (!pieceId) return [];
+        const color = getPieceColor(pieceId), type = getPieceType(pieceId);
+        const moves = [];
+
+        const addMove = (nr, nc) => {
+            if (nr >= 0 && nr < 10 && nc >= 0 && nc < 9) {
+                const tgt = state.board[nr][nc];
+                if (!tgt || getPieceColor(tgt) !== color) moves.push({r: nr, c: nc});
+            }
+        };
+
+        if (type === 'che') {
+            for (let i=r-1; i>=0; i--) { addMove(i,c); if(state.board[i][c]) break; }
+            for (let i=r+1; i<10; i++) { addMove(i,c); if(state.board[i][c]) break; }
+            for (let i=c-1; i>=0; i--) { addMove(r,i); if(state.board[r][i]) break; }
+            for (let i=c+1; i<9; i++) { addMove(r,i); if(state.board[r][i]) break; }
+        } else if (type === 'ma') {
+            [{dr:-2,dc:-1,br:-1,bc:0},{dr:-2,dc:1,br:-1,bc:0},{dr:2,dc:-1,br:1,bc:0},{dr:2,dc:1,br:1,bc:0},
+             {dr:-1,dc:-2,br:0,bc:-1},{dr:1,dc:-2,br:0,bc:-1},{dr:-1,dc:2,br:0,bc:1},{dr:1,dc:2,br:0,bc:1}]
+            .forEach(d => {
+                if(r+d.br>=0 && r+d.br<10 && c+d.bc>=0 && c+d.bc<9 && !state.board[r+d.br][c+d.bc]) addMove(r+d.dr, c+d.dc);
+            });
+        } else if (type === 'xiang') {
+            [{dr:-2,dc:-2,br:-1,bc:-1},{dr:-2,dc:2,br:-1,bc:1},{dr:2,dc:-2,br:1,bc:-1},{dr:2,dc:2,br:1,bc:1}]
+            .forEach(d => {
+                const nr = r+d.dr, nc = c+d.dc;
+                if((color==='red' && nr<5) || (color==='black' && nr>4)) return;
+                if(r+d.br>=0 && r+d.br<10 && c+d.bc>=0 && c+d.bc<9 && !state.board[r+d.br][c+d.bc]) addMove(nr, nc);
+            });
+        } else if (type === 'shi') {
+            [{dr:-1,dc:-1},{dr:-1,dc:1},{dr:1,dc:-1},{dr:1,dc:1}].forEach(d => {
+                const nr=r+d.dr, nc=c+d.dc;
+                if(nc>=3 && nc<=5 && ((color==='red' && nr>=7 && nr<=9) || (color==='black' && nr>=0 && nr<=2))) addMove(nr, nc);
+            });
+        } else if (type === 'jiang') {
+            [{dr:-1,dc:0},{dr:1,dc:0},{dr:0,dc:-1},{dr:0,dc:1}].forEach(d => {
+                const nr=r+d.dr, nc=c+d.dc;
+                if(nc>=3 && nc<=5 && ((color==='red' && nr>=7 && nr<=9) || (color==='black' && nr>=0 && nr<=2))) addMove(nr, nc);
+            });
+        } else if (type === 'pao') {
+            const calcLine = (dr, dc) => {
+                let jump = false, cr = r+dr, cc = c+dc;
+                while(cr>=0 && cr<10 && cc>=0 && cc<9) {
+                    if(!state.board[cr][cc]) { if(!jump) addMove(cr,cc); }
+                    else {
+                        if(!jump) jump = true;
+                        else { if(getPieceColor(state.board[cr][cc]) !== color) addMove(cr,cc); break; }
+                    }
+                    cr+=dr; cc+=dc;
+                }
+            };
+            calcLine(-1,0); calcLine(1,0); calcLine(0,-1); calcLine(0,1);
+        } else if (type === 'bing') {
+            const dr = color === 'red' ? -1 : 1;
+            addMove(r+dr, c);
+            if((color==='red' && r<5) || (color==='black' && r>4)) { addMove(r, c-1); addMove(r, c+1); }
+        }
+        return moves;
+    }
+
+    // --- 交互与执行 ---
+
+    function handleBoardClick(e) {
+        if (state.gameOver) return;
+        // 人机模式下，非玩家回合禁止操作
+        if (state.mode === 'ai' && state.turn !== 'red') return;
+
+        const rect = UI.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
+        const scaleX = UI.canvas.width / (rect.width * (window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
+        const scaleY = UI.canvas.height / (rect.height * (window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
+        
+        const x = (e.clientX - rect.left) * scaleX;
+        const y = (e.clientY - rect.top) * scaleY;
+
+        const renderC = Math.round((x - CONFIG.offsetX) / CONFIG.cellSize);
+        const renderR = Math.round((y - CONFIG.offsetY) / CONFIG.cellSize);
+
+        if (renderR < 0 || renderR > 9 || renderC < 0 || renderC > 8) return;
+
+        // 转换为逻辑坐标
+        const { r, c } = getLogicPos(renderR, renderC);
+        const piece = state.board[r][c];
+
+        if (state.selectedPiece) {
+            const sr = state.selectedPiece.r, sc = state.selectedPiece.c;
+            if (state.validMoves.some(m => m.r === r && m.c === c)) {
+                executeMove(sr, sc, r, c);
+                return;
+            }
+        }
+
+        // 只能选中当前回合颜色的棋子
+        if (piece && getPieceColor(piece) === state.turn) {
+            state.selectedPiece = {r, c};
+            state.validMoves = calculateValidMoves(r, c);
+        } else {
+            state.selectedPiece = null;
+            state.validMoves = [];
+        }
+    }
+
+    function executeMove(sr, sc, dr, dc) {
+        saveHistory();
+        
+        const piece = state.board[sr][sc];
+        const target = state.board[dr][dc];
+        
+        state.board[dr][dc] = piece;
+        state.board[sr][sc] = null;
+        
+        // 能量
+        let gain = target ? 20 : 10;
+        if (state.turn === 'red') state.redEnergy = Math.min(100, state.redEnergy + gain);
+        else state.blackEnergy = Math.min(100, state.blackEnergy + gain);
+
+        state.selectedPiece = null;
+        state.validMoves = [];
+
+        // 胜负判定
+        if (target === 'b_jiang') { endGame('red'); return; }
+        if (target === 'r_jiang') { endGame('black'); return; }
+
+        state.turn = state.turn === 'red' ? 'black' : 'red';
+        updateUI();
+
+        if (state.mode === 'ai' && state.turn === 'black' && !state.gameOver) {
+            setTimeout(playAITurn, 600);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // --- AI 逻辑 ---
+
+    function playAITurn() {
+        if (state.gameOver) return;
+
+        if (state.blackEnergy >= 100) {
+            useSkill('black');
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const moves = [];
+        for (let r=0; r<10; r++) {
+            for (let c=0; c<9; c++) {
+                if (getPieceColor(state.board[r][c]) === 'black') {
+                    calculateValidMoves(r, c).forEach(m => {
+                        let score = 0;
+                        const t = state.board[m.r][m.c];
+                        if (t) {
+                            const ty = getPieceType(t);
+                            if(ty==='jiang') score+=1000; else if(ty==='che') score+=90; else if(ty==='pao'||ty==='ma') score+=45; else score+=15;
+                        }
+                        score += Math.random() * 5;
+                        moves.push({sr: r, sc: c, dr: m.r, dc: m.c, score});
+                    });
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (moves.length > 0) {
+            moves.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
+            executeMove(moves[0].sr, moves[0].sc, moves[0].dr, moves[0].dc);
+        }
+    }
+
+    // --- 绝招系统 ---
+
+    function useSkill(color) {
+        if (state.gameOver || state.turn !== color) return;
+        if (color === 'red' && state.redEnergy < 100) return;
+        if (color === 'black' && state.blackEnergy < 100) return;
+
+        const myPieces = [], enemyPieces = [];
+        for (let r=0; r<10; r++) {
+            for (let c=0; c<9; c++) {
+                const p = state.board[r][c];
+                if (p && getPieceType(p) !== 'jiang') {
+                    if (getPieceColor(p) === color) myPieces.push({r, c});
+                    else enemyPieces.push({r, c});
+                }
+            }
+        }
+
+        if (myPieces.length > 0 && enemyPieces.length > 0) {
+            saveHistory();
+            if (color === 'red') state.redEnergy = 0; else state.blackEnergy = 0;
+            
+            const sac = myPieces[Math.floor(Math.random() * myPieces.length)];
+            const tgt = enemyPieces[Math.floor(Math.random() * enemyPieces.length)];
+            
+            state.board[sac.r][sac.c] = null;
+            state.board[tgt.r][tgt.c] = null;
+            
+            playSkillAnimation(color === 'red' ? '天地同寿!' : '暗影突袭!', color === 'red' ? '#ef4444' : '#94a3b8');
+            
+            state.turn = state.turn === 'red' ? 'black' : 'red';
+            updateUI();
+            
+            if (state.mode === 'ai' && state.turn === 'black') {
+                setTimeout(playAITurn, 1500);
+            }
+        } else {
+            showMessage("场上无可用祭品或目标！", 1000);
+        }
+    }
+
+    function playSkillAnimation(text, color) {
+        const w = UI.effectCanvas.width, h = UI.effectCanvas.height;
+        let frame = 0;
+        function anim() {
+            UI.effectCtx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
+            if (frame < 60) {
+                const alpha = frame < 10 ? frame/10 : (frame > 50 ? (60-frame)/10 : 1);
+                const scale = 1 + (frame/60)*0.5;
+                UI.effectCtx.save();
+                UI.effectCtx.globalAlpha = alpha;
+                UI.effectCtx.translate(w/2, h/2);
+                UI.effectCtx.scale(scale, scale);
+                UI.effectCtx.font = "bold 40px Arial";
+                UI.effectCtx.textAlign = "center"; UI.effectCtx.textBaseline = "middle";
+                UI.effectCtx.fillStyle = color; UI.effectCtx.shadowColor = color; UI.effectCtx.shadowBlur = 20;
+                UI.effectCtx.fillText(text, 0, 0);
+                UI.effectCtx.restore();
+                frame++; requestAnimationFrame(anim);
+            } else { UI.effectCtx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h); }
+        }
+        anim();
+    }
+
+    // --- 界面与渲染 ---
+
+    function endGame(winner) {
+        state.gameOver = true;
+        if (winner === 'red') { state.stats.wins++; showMessage("红方 胜！", 3000); }
+        else { state.stats.losses++; showMessage("黑方 胜！", 3000); }
+        saveStats(); updateUI();
+    }
+
+    function showMessage(text, duration) {
+        UI.msg.textContent = text;
+        UI.msg.className = 'show-message';
+        setTimeout(() => { UI.msg.className = ''; }, duration);
+    }
+
+    function updateUI() {
+        document.getElementById('red-energy').style.width = state.redEnergy + '%';
+        document.getElementById('black-energy').style.width = state.blackEnergy + '%';
+        document.getElementById('red-energy-text').textContent = state.redEnergy + '/100';
+        document.getElementById('black-energy-text').textContent = state.blackEnergy + '/100';
+
+        const setSkillBtn = (btn, en, isActiveTurn) => {
+            if (en >= 100 && isActiveTurn) { btn.disabled = false; btn.classList.add('ready'); }
+            else { btn.disabled = true; btn.classList.remove('ready'); }
+        };
+
+        setSkillBtn(UI.btnRedSkill, state.redEnergy, state.turn === 'red');
+        setSkillBtn(UI.btnBlackSkill, state.blackEnergy, state.turn === 'black' && state.mode !== 'ai');
+
+        const rc = document.getElementById('red-card'), bc = document.getElementById('black-card');
+        if (state.turn === 'red') { rc.classList.add('active'); bc.classList.remove('active'); }
+        else { bc.classList.add('active'); rc.classList.remove('active'); }
+    }
+
+    function renderLoop() {
+        if (UI.game.style.display !== 'none') drawBoard();
+        requestAnimationFrame(renderLoop);
+    }
+
+    // 绘制炮/兵的位置十字标记
+    function drawCrossMarks(ox, oy, cs) {
+        UI.ctx.strokeStyle = CONFIG.colors.gridLine;
+        UI.ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
+
+        const drawMark = (logicR, logicC, hideLeft=false, hideRight=false) => {
+            const { r: renderR, c: renderC } = getRenderPos(logicR, logicC);
+            const x = ox + renderC * cs;
+            const y = oy + renderR * cs;
+            const o = cs * 0.08; // gap
+            const l = cs * 0.15; // len
+
+            UI.ctx.beginPath();
+            if (!hideRight) {
+                UI.ctx.moveTo(x+o, y-o-l); UI.ctx.lineTo(x+o, y-o); UI.ctx.lineTo(x+o+l, y-o);
+                UI.ctx.moveTo(x+o, y+o+l); UI.ctx.lineTo(x+o, y+o); UI.ctx.lineTo(x+o+l, y+o);
+            }
+            if (!hideLeft) {
+                UI.ctx.moveTo(x-o, y-o-l); UI.ctx.lineTo(x-o, y-o); UI.ctx.lineTo(x-o-l, y-o);
+                UI.ctx.moveTo(x-o, y+o+l); UI.ctx.lineTo(x-o, y+o); UI.ctx.lineTo(x-o-l, y+o);
+            }
+            UI.ctx.stroke();
+        };
+
+        // 炮
+        drawMark(2, 1); drawMark(2, 7); drawMark(7, 1); drawMark(7, 7);
+        // 黑卒
+        drawMark(3, 0, true, false); drawMark(3, 2); drawMark(3, 4); drawMark(3, 6); drawMark(3, 8, false, true);
+        // 红兵
+        drawMark(6, 0, true, false); drawMark(6, 2); drawMark(6, 4); drawMark(6, 6); drawMark(6, 8, false, true);
+    }
+
+    function drawBoard() {
+        const w = UI.canvas.width, h = UI.canvas.height;
+        UI.ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
+        
+        const { offsetX: ox, offsetY: oy, cellSize: cs } = CONFIG;
+
+        // 绘制网格
+        UI.ctx.strokeStyle = CONFIG.colors.gridLine;
+        UI.ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
+        UI.ctx.shadowBlur = 5;
+        UI.ctx.shadowColor = CONFIG.colors.gridLine;
+
+        UI.ctx.beginPath();
+        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { UI.ctx.moveTo(ox, oy + i*cs); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + 8*cs, oy + i*cs); }
+        for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) {
+            if (i === 0 || i === 8) { UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + i*cs, oy); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + i*cs, oy + 9*cs); }
+            else { UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + i*cs, oy); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + i*cs, oy + 4*cs); UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + i*cs, oy + 5*cs); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + i*cs, oy + 9*cs); }
+        }
+        
+        // 九宫格
+        UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + 3*cs, oy); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + 5*cs, oy + 2*cs);
+        UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + 5*cs, oy); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + 3*cs, oy + 2*cs);
+        UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + 3*cs, oy + 7*cs); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + 5*cs, oy + 9*cs);
+        UI.ctx.moveTo(ox + 5*cs, oy + 7*cs); UI.ctx.lineTo(ox + 3*cs, oy + 9*cs);
+        UI.ctx.stroke();
+        UI.ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
+
+        // 楚河汉界文字 (根据翻转调整)
+        UI.ctx.fillStyle = CONFIG.colors.boardText;
+        UI.ctx.font = `bold ${cs * 0.5}px Arial`;
+        UI.ctx.textAlign = "center"; UI.ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
+        if (state.isFlipped) {
+            UI.ctx.save(); UI.ctx.translate(ox + 4*cs, oy + 4.5*cs); UI.ctx.rotate(Math.PI);
+            UI.ctx.fillText("楚 河", 2*cs, 0); UI.ctx.fillText("汉 界", -2*cs, 0); UI.ctx.restore();
+        } else {
+            UI.ctx.fillText("楚 河", ox + 2*cs, oy + 4.5*cs); UI.ctx.fillText("汉 界", ox + 6*cs, oy + 4.5*cs);
+        }
+
+        // 炮/兵位置十字标记
+        drawCrossMarks(ox, oy, cs);
+
+        // 合法走法高亮
+        if (state.validMoves.length > 0) {
+            UI.ctx.fillStyle = CONFIG.colors.validMove;
+            state.validMoves.forEach(m => {
+                const renderPos = getRenderPos(m.r, m.c);
+                UI.ctx.beginPath(); UI.ctx.arc(ox + renderPos.c*cs, oy + renderPos.r*cs, cs * 0.15, 0, Math.PI * 2); UI.ctx.fill();
+            });
+        }
+
+        // 选中高亮
+        if (state.selectedPiece) {
+            UI.ctx.strokeStyle = CONFIG.colors.select; UI.ctx.lineWidth = 3;
+            const rp = getRenderPos(state.selectedPiece.r, state.selectedPiece.c);
+            UI.ctx.strokeRect(ox + rp.c*cs - cs*0.45, oy + rp.r*cs - cs*0.45, cs*0.9, cs*0.9);
+        }
+
+        // 绘制棋子
+        const pR = cs * 0.42;
+        for (let r = 0; r < 10; r++) {
+            for (let c = 0; c < 9; c++) {
+                const id = state.board[r][c];
+                if (id) {
+                    const p = PIECES[id];
+                    const rp = getRenderPos(r, c);
+                    const x = ox + rp.c*cs, y = oy + rp.r*cs;
+                    
+                    UI.ctx.beginPath(); UI.ctx.arc(x, y, pR, 0, Math.PI*2);
+                    UI.ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b'; UI.ctx.fill();
+                    
+                    UI.ctx.strokeStyle = p.color === 'red' ? CONFIG.colors.red : CONFIG.colors.black;
+                    UI.ctx.lineWidth = 2; UI.ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
+                    UI.ctx.shadowColor = p.color === 'red' ? CONFIG.colors.redGlow : CONFIG.colors.blackGlow;
+                    UI.ctx.stroke(); UI.ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
+
+                    UI.ctx.beginPath(); UI.ctx.arc(x, y, pR*0.8, 0, Math.PI*2);
+                    UI.ctx.strokeStyle = '#334155'; UI.ctx.lineWidth = 1; UI.ctx.stroke();
+
+                    UI.ctx.fillStyle = p.color === 'red' ? CONFIG.colors.red : CONFIG.colors.black;
+                    UI.ctx.font = `bold ${cs * 0.45}px "KaiTi", "STKaiti", serif`;
+                    UI.ctx.textAlign = "center"; UI.ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
+                    
+                    // 如果棋盘翻转，且棋子文字需要正对玩家
+                    UI.ctx.save();
+                    UI.ctx.translate(x, y);
+                    if (state.isFlipped) UI.ctx.rotate(Math.PI);
+                    UI.ctx.fillText(p.text, 0, cs * 0.05);
+                    UI.ctx.restore();
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    window.onload = init;
+})();
+</script>
+</body>
+</html>
\ No newline at end of file
